### marwinsteiner/xwjev

<details><summary>README 발췌</summary>

The NYT daily crossword of Thursday 2026-10-15, solved with every letter correct 3.0 s into the solve on a laptop CPU (retriever-only run; model loading adds 3.4 s). Each frame is one belief-propagation sweep or one accepted local-search move. Pale cells are uncertain.
